⁴⁰⁶ Talmud Bavli, Rosh HaShanah 17a ibid.
⁴⁰⁷ Micah 7:18
⁴⁰⁸ Talmud Bavli, Shabbat 152a
⁴⁰⁹ Proverbs 14:30


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:25)


ואמרו ז"ל (תענית דף כ"ה ע"ב) מעשה ברבי אליעזר שירד לפני התיבה ולא נענה וכו', ירד רבי עקיבא ונענה ויצאה בת קול ואמרה מפני שזה מעביר על מדותיו.


Similarly, our sages, of blessed memory, related,⁴¹⁰ “There was an incident in which Rabbi Eliezer descended before the ark to serve as the prayer leader, but he was not answered. Rabbi Akiva descended before the ark after him, and was answered. The sages whispered amongst themselves about this, and a Supernal voice (*Bat Kol*) emerged and said, ‘It is not because this one is greater than that one, but because this one is tolerant and forgiving and that one is intolerant and unforgiving.’”

footnotes:
⁴¹⁰ Talmud Bavli, Taanit 25b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:26)


ואמרו ז"ל (חולין דף פ"ט ע"א) אין העולם מתקיים אלא במי שבולם פיו בשעת מריבה, שנאמר (איוב כ"ו ז') תולה ארץ על בלימה, רבי אבהו אומר על מי שמשים עצמו כמי שאינו וכו', ותנו רבנן (שבת דף ס"ח ע"א) הנעלבין ואינם עולבין שומעין חרפתן ואינן משיבין עליהם הכתוב אומר (שופטים ה' ל"א) ואוהביו כצאת השמש בגבורתו,


The sages, of blessed memory, also said,⁴¹¹ “The world is only sustained in the merit of those who muzzle (*Bolem*-בולם) their mouths in a time of quarrel, as written,⁴¹² ‘He hangs the earth upon nothingness (*Blimah*-בלימה).’ Rabbi Avahu said, this refers to a person who makes himself as if he is nothingness.” The Rabbis taught,⁴¹³ “Regarding those who are insulted but do not insult, who hear themselves shamed but do not respond, the verse states,⁴¹⁴ ‘Those who love Him are like the sun as it goes forth in its might.’”

footnotes:
⁴¹¹ Talmud Bavli, Chullin 89a (See Rashi)
⁴¹² Job 26:7
⁴¹³ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 23a
⁴¹⁴ Judges 5:31


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:27)


ואמר אליהו זכור לטוב (אליהו זוטא פ"ד) לעולם הוי עלוב לבני אדם ולבני ביתך יותר מכולם. ואמר אבא אליהו זכור לטוב (כלה רבתי ספ"ה) לעולם אין התורה מתפרשת אלא במי שאינו קפדן ואף אני איני נגלה אלא למי שאינו קפדן:


Father Eliyahu, who is remembered for good, said,⁴¹⁵ “The knowledge of Torah is revealed only to one who is not intolerant, and I too will not be revealed except to one who is not intolerant.”

footnotes:
⁴¹⁵ Tractate Kallah Rabbati Ch. 5


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:28)


העצבות גורמת מניעת העבודה וקיום המצות ובטול עסק התורה וכונת התפילה ומבטל מחשבה טובה לעבוד את ה', והיא שער התחלת גרוי הסתת היצר הרע אפילו אם הוא צדיק, בהראותו כי אין לו תועלת בעבודה בהיות יסורים עליו וכו'.


**Depression** obstructs one’s service of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, and the fulfillment of His *mitzvot*. It obstructs one’s involvement in Torah study and having proper devotional intent in prayer and negates his good and positive thoughts to serve *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He. It is the gateway through which the evil inclination entices and turns a person away from serving *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, even if he is a righteous *Tzaddik*, by making it appear to him, that because of his suffering, his service of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, is ineffectual.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:29)


וגם כי בא עליו בדרך חסידות באמור לו איך יעלה בדעתך עפר רמה ותולעה להתקרב להתקדש בקדושת מלכו של עולם, וכמו שכתוב (משלי כ"ה ז') ראמות לאויל חכמות וגו' וכמו שכתוב (איוב ל"ה ז') ואם צדקת מה תתן לו:


Moreover, the evil inclination will even come under the guise of piety and tell him, “How can it even enter your mind that you, who are nothing but dust, worms and maggots, can come close and be sanctified in the holiness of the King of the world! Does not the verse state,⁴¹⁶ ‘For a fool wisdom is unattainable?’” Moreover, the evil inclination says, “Even if you attain righteousness, does it not say,⁴¹⁷ ‘If you are righteous, what have you given Him?’” In addition, depression causes the Holy Spirit (*Ru’ach HaKodesh*) to depart from him.

footnotes:
⁴¹⁶ Proverbs 24:7
⁴¹⁷ Job 35:7


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:30)


גם גורמת סלוק רוח הקודש מעליו, וההפך בהיותו עובד ה' יתברך בשמחה כמו שכתוב (תהלים ק' ב') עבדו את ה' בשמחה, כי השמחה מוסיפה חשק רב ואהבה להתדבק בו יתברך וכתיב (דברים כ"ח מ"ז) תחת אשר לא עבדת את ה' אלהיך בשמחה ובטוב לבב וגו'. כי העבודה אם היא בעצבון דומה לעבד העובד לרבו בפנים עצבות וזועפות.


The opposite is true when one serves *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, with joy, as it states,⁴¹⁸ “Serve *HaShem*-יהו״ה with joy.” For joy increases one’s desire and love of adhering to *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, as written,⁴¹⁹ “Because you did not serve *HaShem*-יהו״ה your God with joy and gladness of heart etc.” For, a person who serves *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, with melancholy, is likened to a servant who serves his master with a sullen and annoyed face.

footnotes:
⁴¹⁸ Psalms 100:2
⁴¹⁹ Deuteronomy 28:47


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:31)


וכתיב (משלי י' כ"ב) ברכת ה' היא תעשיר ולא יוסיף עצב עמה. כי העצבון הוא נמשך מזהמת סמא"ל ונחש שהטילו באדם וחוה כמו שכתוב באדם (בראשית ג' י"ז) בעצבון תאכלנה וכתיב (שם ט"ז) בעצב תלדי בנים,


It is likewise written,⁴²⁰ “The blessing of *HaShem*-יהו״ה is what enriches, and sadness (*Etzev*-עצב) does not increase it.” For, sadness is drawn from the contamination of *Sama’el*⁴²¹ and the snake, who infected Adam and Chavah with it, as it states about Adam,⁴²² “With sadness (*Eetzavon*-**עצב**ון) you shall eat of it all the days of your life,” and about Chava it states,⁴²³ “I will greatly increase your sadness (*Eetzavon*-**עצב**ון)… with sadness (*b’Etzev-*ב**עצב**) you shall bear children.”⁴²⁴

footnotes:
⁴²⁰ Proverbs 10:22 – The root Etzev-עצב of this verse is explained to mean “suffering” or “burdensome toil” (see Rashi), however, the simple meaning of the root Etzev-עצב is “sadness” or “melancholy,” as in the verse (Isaiah 54:6), “For like a wife who is forlorn and melancholy (Atzoovah-עצובה), HaShem-יהו״ה has called you. Can one cast off the wife of his Youth? Said your God.” (See Radak to Isaiah 54:6 ibid.)
⁴²¹  This is the angelic name of Satan-the Accuser.
⁴²² Genesis 3:17
⁴²³ Genesis 3:16
⁴²⁴ As explained in the prior note, the root Etzev-עצב can mean “suffering” or “sadness.” In context to the subject being explained here, it means “sadness.”


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:32)


ועל ידי כך הקדוש ברוך הוא ושכינתו מסתלקים מעליו: והראיה מיעקב בחיר האבות שנתסלקה ממנו שכינה ורוח הקדש עשרים ושתים שנים שפרש יוסף ממנו, וכששמח בבשורתו כתיב ותחי רוח יעקב, ששרתה עליו רוח הקדש.


On account of this, the Holy One, blessed is He, and His Indwelling Presence, the *Shechinah*, withdraw from him. Proof of this is our forefather Yaakov, the choicest of the forefathers,⁴²⁵ from whom the Holy Spirit (*Ru’ach HaKodesh*) departed for a period of twenty-two years when Yosef was separated from him and he assumed that he had been devoured by a wild animal. However, when he rejoiced over the tidings that Yosef was alive, the verse states,⁴²⁶ “The spirit of Yaakov was revived,” that is, the Holy Spirit (*Ru’ach HaKodesh*) returned and rested upon him.⁴²⁷

footnotes:
⁴²⁵ Midrash Rabba 76:1 and elsewhere.
⁴²⁶ Genesis 45:27
⁴²⁷ Avot d’Rabbi Natan 30:4; Midrash Tanchuma, Veyeishev 2:7; Rashi to Genesis 45:7 ibid., and elsewhere.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:33)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (פסחים דף קי"ז ע"א) אין שכינה ואין נבואה שורה לא מתוך עצבות וכו'. וכן מצינו באלישע ובבני הנביאים דכתיב (מלכים ב' ג' ט"ו) והיה כנגן המנגן וכתיב (שמואל א' י' ה') ולפניהם תוף וחליל וכנור והמה מתנבאים,


Similarly, our sages,⁴²⁸ of blessed memory, taught that the Indwelling Presence of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, (the *Shechinah*) and the spirit of Prophecy, do not dwell in a place of sadness. We thus find that it says about Elisha and the students of the prophets,⁴²⁹ “As the musician played, the spirit of *HaShem*-יהו״ה came upon him,” and it states,⁴³⁰ “When you arrive at the city, you will encounter a group of prophets coming down from the high place, and leading them will be players of lyre, drum, flute and harp, and they will be prophesying.”

footnotes:
⁴²⁸ Talmud Bavli, Pesachim 117a; Zohar I 217b
⁴²⁹ Kings II 3:15
⁴³⁰ Samuel I 10:5


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:34)


ואפילו בכל עבודת מצוה ותהלה צריך שיהיה בתכלית השמחה כמו שמצינו באביי (ברכות דף ל' ע"א) דהוה בדיח טובא ואמר תפילין קא מנחנא. וכן רב ברונא משום דסמך גאולה לתפלה לא פסק חוכא מפומיה כל ההוא יומא:


Even when serving *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, in the performance of any *mitzvah* or in prayer, one must be in an ultimate state of joy. This can be seen from Abaye,⁴³¹ who was sitting before his teacher Rabbah and was excessively joyful, and when questioned about his joy, he said, “I now am donned in *Tefillin*.” It is similarly related⁴³² that, “Rav Bruna was a great man who rejoiced in the *mitzvot*. Once, through juxtaposing the blessing of redemption (*Ge’ulah*) to the Amidah prayer, a smile did not depart from his mouth for that entire day.”

footnotes:
⁴³¹ Talmud Bavli, Brachot 30b
⁴³² Talmud Bavli, Brachot 9b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:35)


שנאה, על ידי כך חוטא לחבירו להרע לו בכל מיני רעה. ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ד) שנאת הבריות מוציאה את האדם מן העולם, והנה שונא כביכול להקדוש ברוך הוא על שברא אותו, והנה כולנו בנים לה' אלהינו, ושונא את אברהם יצחק ויעקב שיצא מהם זה. והנה כל הנשמות נאחזות בשם יתברך וצורך גבוה הם, אשר בהם יתפאר ואיך תשנאהו:


**Hatred** brings a person to sin against his fellow man, by inflicting all types of evil upon him. Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴³³ “Hating the creatures removes a person from the world,” for by doing so, it is as if he harbors hatred of their Creator, the Holy One, blessed is He, who created him, for are we not all children to *HaShem*-יהו״ה our God?⁴³⁴ Moreover, by hating another Jew, it is as if he also hates Avraham, Yitzchak and Yaakov, from whom this person came forth. All souls are bound to the Name *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, and each has an extremely lofty purpose, namely that through them *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, will be glorified.⁴³⁵ This being so, how can anyone hate his fellow?

footnotes:
⁴³³ Mishnah Avot 2:11
⁴³⁴ Deuteronomy 14:1
⁴³⁵ Isaiah 49:3


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:36)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (יומא דף ט' ע"ב) בית שני היו בו צדיקים וחכמים גדולים ולא נחרב אלא בעון שנאת חנם ולא נתארך הקץ ולא נעלם אלא לסיבת שנאת חנם, ולא עוד, ששאר העבירות אינו עובר עליהם אלא בשעתם אבל שנאת חנם בלב, היא תמיד, ובכל רגע עובר על לא תשנא, ובטול מצות עשה (ויקרא י"ח י"ט) ואהבת לרעך כמוך,


Our sages, of blessed memory, related⁴³⁶ that during the period of the second Holy Temple, there were many righteous *Tzaddikim* and Torah Scholars. Nonetheless, the Holy Temple was destroyed only because of the sin of baseless hatred. They likewise said that the exile was lengthened and its end was concealed solely due to baseless hatred. Furthermore, in regard to all other sins, a person is only in a state of transgression when he actively violates them, whereas baseless hatred is a constant transgression that one carries in his heart. Therefore, he is in violation of the negative *mitzvah*,⁴³⁷ “You shall not hate your brother in your heart” and negates the positive *mitzvah* of,⁴³⁸ “You shall love your fellow as yourself,” at every moment.

footnotes:
⁴³⁶ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 9b
⁴³⁷ Leviticus 19:17
⁴³⁸ Leviticus 19:18


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:37)


ולא עוד אלא שעל מצוה זו אמרו (תו"כ פ' קדושים) שזה כלל גדול שבתורה שכולה תלויה בה, ולא זכה משה לכל מעלותיו אלא שהיה אוהב לישראל ומצטער בצרתם כמו שאמרו ז"ל (מדרש רבה שמות פ"א) על וירא בסבלותם, ועל פסוק (שמות ל"ב ל"ב) ועתה אם תשא חטאתם ואם אין מחני נא, ולכן היה שקול ככל ישראל כמו שכתוב (ישעיה ס"ג י"א) ויזכור ימי עולם משה עמו:


Furthermore, about the *mitzvah* of loving one’s fellow Jew, they stated,⁴³⁹ “This is a great principle that includes the entire Torah in it,” for everything depends on it. Our teacher Moshe only merited attaining all of his wondrous and exalted levels because of his great love of Israel and because he was pained by their suffering, as our sages, of blessed memory,⁴⁴⁰ said about the verse,⁴⁴¹ “He saw their burdens,” and about the verse,⁴⁴² “Now, if You would but forgive their sin – but if not, erase me now from Your book that You have written.” On account of this, he was equated to the entire Jewish people, as written,⁴⁴³ “They remembered the days of old, of Moshe, His people.”

footnotes:
⁴³⁹ Rashi to Leviticus 19:18, citing Sifra; Torat Kohanim, and elsewhere.
⁴⁴⁰ See Rashi to Exodus 2:11, citing Midrash Shemot Rabba 1:27
⁴⁴¹ Exodus 2:11
⁴⁴² Exodus 32:32
⁴⁴³ Isaiah 63:11; Also see Rashi to Exodus 18:1 citing Mechilta.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:38)


הקנאה, היא גרמא לבא לידי שנאה, וגם היא יותר קשה כי מורד על הקדוש ברוך הוא למה השפיע טובה לחבירו יותר ממנו, ואמרו ז"ל (אבות פ"ד כ"א) הקנאה והתאוה והכבוד מוציאין את האדם מן העולם,


**Envy** brings a person to hatred and is actually even worse than hatred, since by being envious, he rebels against the Holy One, blessed is He, by saying to himself, “Why has the Holy One, blessed is He, bestowed more goodness to my fellow than to me?” Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁴⁴ “Envy, lust, and [the desire for] honor, remove a person from the world.”

footnotes:
⁴⁴⁴ Mishnah Avot 4:21


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:39)


צא ולמד כי כל הראשונים לא נענשו אלא בשביל הקנאה, כי הנה קין המדבר פה אל פה עם השם יתברך נטרד מן העולם הוא וזרעיותיו על שקנא לתאומתו של הבל ועל ידי כן עבר כל אותם העבירות, ועשרת השבטים לא גלו למצרים אלא בעון ויקנאו בו אחיו וסוף סוף נענשו בעשרה הרוגי מלכות, וירבעם שכל חכמי דורו לפניו כעשבי השדה נטרד מן העולם בקנאתו ממלכי בית דוד,


Study and you will see that all the ancients were punished solely on account of their envy. Cain, who spoke with *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, mouth to mouth, was excised from the world along with his descendants, because he was envious of his brother Abel’s twin sister.⁴⁴⁵ As a result of his jealousy he came to transgress all his sins. Similarly, the ten tribes of Israel were exiled to Egypt because Joseph’s brothers envied him.⁴⁴⁶ Ultimately, they were further punished with the incident the ten martyrs.⁴⁴⁷ Yeravam, before whom all the Torah scholars of the generation were like grass of the field,⁴⁴⁸ was excised from the world solely because he envied the kingdom of the House of David.

footnotes:
⁴⁴⁵ Midrash Bereishit Rabba 22:1; Pirkei d’Rabbi Eliezer, Ch. 21
⁴⁴⁶ Genesis 37:11; Also see Tanchuma Veyeishev 18 and elsewhere.
⁴⁴⁷ Midrash Eileh Ezkera and elsewhere.
⁴⁴⁸ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 101a-b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:40)


גם שאול בחיר השם כבן שנה שלא טעם טעם חטא ולא היה במלכותו שום דופי כמו שאמרו ז"ל (יומא דף כ"ב ע"ב), נטרד ממלכותו ונהרג הוא ושלשת בניו ונהרגו נוב עיר הכהנים וגלו ישראל ביד פלשתים ונחרב משכן שילה, והכל על שקינא לדוד בענין גולית סרה מעליו רוח הקדש ונחה עליו רוח עועים:


Even Shaul, the chosen one of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, who as taught by our sages, of blessed memory, was as pure as a one-year-old child who has never tasted sin⁴⁴⁹ and whose ancestry and kingship were free of flaw,⁴⁵⁰ was excised from his kingship and killed along with his three sons, for having caused the people of Nov, the city of the Priests, to be killed, for having caused the exile of the Jewish people amongst the Philistines, and for having caused the destruction of the Tabernacle (*Mishkan*) at Shiloh, all of which were brought about because he envied David regarding the matter of Goliath.⁴⁵¹ This caused the Holy Spirit (*Ru’ach HaKodesh*) to withdraw from him, which was replaced by a spirit of distortion (*Ru’ach Eeveem*)⁴⁵² that rested upon him.

footnotes:
⁴⁴⁹ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 22b
⁴⁵⁰ See Yoma 22b ibid.
⁴⁵¹ Samuel I 18:7 and on.
⁴⁵² Samuel I 18:12; 16:14-15 and elsewhere; Isaiah 19:14


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:41)


ואין בן דוד בא עד שתוסר הקנאה כמו שכתוב (ישעיה י"א י"ג) אפרים לא יקנא את יהודה, ולא עוד כי אפילו מלאכי מרום נפלו מקדושתם על זה כמו שכתוב בפרקי דרבי אליעזר (פרק י"ג-י"ד) על סמא"ל שהיה גדול מן השרפים שאין להם רק שש כנפים והוא היו לו שנים עשר כנפים ונעקר מן הקדושה בשביל קנאה על האדם, וכן הנפילים עז"א ועזא"ל נטרדו מן העולם על שנתקנאו באדם,


Moshiach, the son of David, will not come until the spirit of envy is removed, as written,⁴⁵³ “The jealousy of Ephraim shall cease and the oppressors of Yehudah shall be cut off; Ephraim shall not be jealous of Yehudah and Yehudah will not harass Ephraim.” Moreover, even the Supernal angels fell from their holiness as the result of envy, as written in Pirkei d’Rabbi Eliezer,⁴⁵⁴ that the angel *Sama’el* was greater than all the *Seraphim* angels, in that they only had six wings, whereas he had twelve wings. He nonetheless was uprooted from the side of holiness because he was envious of man. The same is said of the *Nefillim* angels, *Aza* and *Aza’el*, who were excised from the world because they were jealous of man.⁴⁵⁵

footnotes:
⁴⁵³ Isaiah 11:13
⁴⁵⁴ Pirkei d’Rabbi Eliezer, Ch. 13-14
⁴⁵⁵ Pesikta Rabbati 34:1; Yoma 67b and Rashi there; Zohar I 37a.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:42)


וכל מי שיש בו קנאה עצמותיו מרקיבין, ולכן ראוי לאדם להיות שמח בחלקו אף אם מעט הוא, כי לא טוב הוא מהלל הזקן ורבי חנינא בן דוסא ורבי יהודה בר אלעאי ורבי אלעזר בן פדת וזולתם:


The bones of whoever possesses envy shall rot.⁴⁵⁶ It therefore is appropriate for a person to be happy with his lot,⁴⁵⁷ even if it is small. For, he is certainly no better than Hillel the Elder, Rabbi Chaninah ben Dosa, Rabbi Yehuda bar Ila’i, Rabbi Elazar ben Pedat, and others like them.⁴⁵⁸

footnotes:
⁴⁵⁶ Proverbs 14:30; Talmud Bavli, Shabbat 152b
⁴⁵⁷ Mishnah Avot 4:1
⁴⁵⁸ All of whom were materially poor. (See Yoma 35b; Brachot 17b; Nedarim 49b; Taanit 25a, and elsewhere.)


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:43)


החמדה, אב הטומאה, כי היא מביאה לשנאה וקנאה ומביאה לידי גזל ושבועת שקר ולידי רציחה, והיא העשירית שבעשרת הדברות שקולה ככולם, כי עינו לא תשבע עושר כעין הנחש שנאמר בו (ישעיה ס"ה כ"ה) ונחש עפר לחמו, וכתיב (משלי כ"ח כ"ב) נבהל להון איש רע עין וגו',


**Greed**is the father of all impurity. It brings to hatred and envy, as well as theft, false oaths and even murder. This prohibition is the last of the Ten Commandments and is equal to them all, since⁴⁵⁹ “his eye is never sated with wealth,” like the eye of the snake, about whom it states,⁴⁶⁰ “The food of the snake shall be dust.” Similarly, it is written,⁴⁶¹ “One who is overeager for wealth has an evil eye, he does not know that want may befall him.”

footnotes:
⁴⁵⁹ Ecclesiastes 4:8
⁴⁶⁰ Isaiah 65:25
⁴⁶¹ Proverbs 28:22


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:44)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל התאוה מוציאה את האדם מן העולם כי מטרידתו מעסק התורה ומקיום מצותיה, והוא כופר בהשגחה שאינו מאמין שהכל על ידי השגחת השם יתברך וכמו שאמרו ז"ל (סוטה דף מ"ח ע"ב) כל מי שיש לו פת בסלו ואומר מה אוכל למחר הרי זה מקטני אמנה ועליו אמר (זכריה ד') כי מי בז ליום קטנות, מי גרם לצדיקים שיתבזבז שלחנן לעתיד לבא, קטנות אמנה שהיתה בהן:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁶² “Lust removes a person from the world,” since it causes his mind to be preoccupied and distracted, thus uprooting him from studying *HaShem’s-*יהו"ה Torah and fulfilling His *mitzvot*. Moreover, he denies *HaShem*’s-יהו״ה Divine Providence, in that he does not believe that everything comes about according to *HaShem’s*-יהו״ה Supernal will and supervision, blessed is He. As our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁶³ “Whoever has bread in his basket and says to himself, ‘What will I eat tomorrow,’ has little faith. About such a person the verse states,⁴⁶⁴ ‘For who wastes the day? Small things.’ That is, what is it that causes the reward of the righteous to be wasted from the coming future? It is their small-mindedness and their smallness of faith in the Holy One, blessed is He.”

footnotes:
⁴⁶² Mishnah Avot 4:21
⁴⁶³ Talmud Bavli, Sotah 48b
⁴⁶⁴ Zachariah 4:10


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:45)


אבל הבטחון אין מעלה כמוהו וכמו שכתוב (תהלים ב' י"ב) אשרי כל חוסי בו, כי מאמין שיש בעולם אלוה יכול ומשגיח, וכתיב (ירמיה י"ז ז') ברוך הגבר אשר יבטח בה' והיה ה' מבטחו, ומובטח הוא שלא יחסרו מזונותיו כמו שכתוב (תהלים נ"ה נ"ג) השלך על ה' יהבך והוא יכלכלך, ובתנאי שיהיה באמת ובכל לבו בלי שום פקפוק, ועוד כי בהיותו בוטח בה' יעסוק בתורה ובמצות בכל לבו בלי הרהור כלל:


In contrast, there is no greater quality than trust in *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, as written,⁴⁶⁵ “Happy are all who trust in Him.” A person who has this quality is faithful that there is a God in the world, who is all-capable and supervises over everything. It thus is written,⁴⁶⁶ “Blessed is the man who trusts in *HaShem*-יהו״ה, *HaShem*-יהו״ה will be his security.” Such a person is certain that he will not lack sustenance, as written,⁴⁶⁷ “Cast your burden upon *HaShem*-יהו״ה and He will sustain you.” However, this applies on condition that his trust in *HaShem*-יהו״ה is truly sincere, with all his heart and without doubt. This is evident by the fact that if he truly trusts in *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, he will occupy himself in studying His Torah and fulfilling His *mitzvot* with all his heart and without any misgivings.

footnotes:
⁴⁶⁵ Psalms 2:12
⁴⁶⁶ Jeremiah 17:7
⁴⁶⁷ Psalms 55:23


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:46)


תאות התענוגים, רעה מכל הנזכרים לעיל, כי בהיותו גרגרן יבא לידי חמדה וגזל, שבועת שקר, שנאת הבריות, וקנאה, ודומה לעשו שנאמר בו (בראשית כ"ה ל') הלעיטני נא, ועליו נאמר (משלי כ"ג ב') ושמת סכין בלועך אם בעל נפש אתה, כי על ידי כן יאכל נבלות וטרפות וכל מאכלות אסורות, וכתיב (קהלת ו' ז') כל עמל האדם לפיהו, וכתיב (משלי י"ג כ"ה) ובטן רשעים תחסר,


**Lust** for pleasure is worse than all the negative traits mentioned above. For, if a person is gluttonous, he will come to fall to greed, theft, false oaths, hatred of the creatures and envy. Such a person is like Esav, who said,⁴⁶⁸ “Please pour some of that red red stuff into me.” About such a person it states,⁴⁶⁹ “If you care for your soul, it would be better for you to put a knife to your throat.” For out of gluttony he will eventually come to eat the meat of an animal that died without being slaughtered (*Neveilah*) or that has been slaughtered improperly (*Treifah*) and all other kinds of forbidden foods. Similarly, it is written,⁴⁷⁰ “All man’s toil is for his mouth,” and it states,⁴⁷¹ “The stomach of the wicked will always be lacking.”

footnotes:
⁴⁶⁸ Genesis 25:30
⁴⁶⁹ Proverbs 23:2 (See Rashi)
⁴⁷⁰ Ecclesiastes 6:7
⁴⁷¹ Proverbs 13:25


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:47)


וכתיב (דברים ל"ב ט"ו) וישמן ישורון ויבעט, ויטוש אלוה עשהו כי מביאו לכחש בשם יתברך, כמו שאמר הכתוב (משלי ל' ט') פן אשבע וכחשתי ואמרתי מי ה',


It likewise is written,⁴⁷² “Yeshurun became fat and kicked. You became fat, you became thick, you became corpulent. He deserted God, his Maker and scorned the Rock of his salvation.” That is, this negative trait brings a person to deny *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, as the verse states,⁴⁷³ “Lest I become sated and deny, saying, ‘Who is *HaShem*-יהו״ה?’”

footnotes:
⁴⁷² Deuteronomy 32:15
⁴⁷³ Proverbs 30:9


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:48)


ועל כל העבירות אין הקטן נענש ובן סורר ומורה שאכל תרטימר בשר וחצי לוג יין, נסקל. ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (תענית דף י"א ע"א) מנהג הרשעים אכול בשר ושתה יין וכו' וכתיב (ישעיה כ"ב י"ד) אם יכופר העון הזה לכם עד תמותון, ואמר אליהו זכור לטוב לרבי יהודה (ברכות כ"ט ע"א) לא תרוי ולא תחטא:


Furthermore, in regard to all other sins, a minor is not punished, however, in the case of the rebellious son, if he steals and eats a *tartimer* of meat and a half *log* of wine, he is stoned to death.⁴⁷⁴ Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁷⁵ “The wicked are accustomed to eat meat and drink wine, saying,⁴⁷⁶ ‘Come, I will fetch wine and we will fill ourselves with strong drink; and tomorrow will be like today etc.’” However, about this it is written,⁴⁷⁷ “Until the day of your death you will not be atoned of this sin.” Eliyahu, who is remembered for good, said to Rabbi Yehudah,⁴⁷⁸ “Do not get drunk and you will not sin.”

footnotes:
⁴⁷⁴ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 70a; Rashi to Deuteronomy 21:18
⁴⁷⁵ Talmud Bavli, Taanit 11a
⁴⁷⁶ Isaiah 56:12
⁴⁷⁷ Isaiah 22:14 – The prior verse states, “Yet behold! There is joy and gladness, slaying of cattle and slaughtering of sheep, eating meat and drinking wine, [saying,] ‘Eat and drink, for tomorrow we die.’
⁴⁷⁸ Talmud Bavli, Brachot 29b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:49)


אבל המסתפק בהכרחי נקרא צדיק כמו שאמר הכתוב (משלי י"ג כ"ה) צדיק אוכל לשובע נפשו, ודוד אמר (תהלים קי"ט ע"א) טוב לי כי עניתי למען אלמד חקיך, ולא שאל יעקב אלא (בראשית כ"ח כ') ונתן לי לחם לאכול ובגד ללבוש, וכתיב (בראשית מ"ט י"ד) יששכר חמור גרם וכו' ויט שכמו לסבול.


In contrast, a person who suffices with what is necessary for him is called a righteous *Tzaddik*, as scripture states,⁴⁷⁹ “A righteous person eats to satisfy his soul.” Similarly, King David said,⁴⁸⁰ “It is good for me that I was afflicted, so that I might learn Your statutes.” We likewise find that our forefather Yaakov only requested the following,⁴⁸¹ “Give me bread to eat and clothes to wear.” It is likewise written,⁴⁸² “Yissachar is a strong-boned donkey… he bent his shoulder to bear.”

footnotes:
⁴⁷⁹ Proverbs 13:25
⁴⁸⁰ Psalms 119:71
⁴⁸¹ Genesis 28:20
⁴⁸² Genesis 49:14


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:50)


ואמרו ז"ל (אבות פ"ו משנה ד') כך היא דרכה של תורה, פת במלח תאכל וכו' אם עשית כן אשריך בעולם הזה וטוב לך לעולם הבא, ואמרו ז"ל (ברכות ס"ג ע"א) אין התורה מתקיימת אלא במי שממית עצמו עליה. ואמרו ז"ל (אבות פ"ב משנה ז') מרבה בשר מרבה רמה.


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁸³ “Such is the way of [the life] of Torah: You shall eat bread with salt and drink rationed water; you shall sleep on the ground and your life will be one of privation, and you shall labor in Torah. If you do this,⁴⁸⁴ ‘You shall be happy and it shall be good for you.’ That is, ‘You shall be happy’ in this world, ‘and it shall be good for you’ in the coming world.” Similarly, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁸⁵ “The words of Torah are only sustained in one who kills himself over them.” They also said,⁴⁸⁶ “Whoever increases his flesh, increases his maggots.”

footnotes:
⁴⁸³ Mishnah Avot 6:4
⁴⁸⁴ Psalms 128:2
⁴⁸⁵ Talmud Bavli, Brachot 63a
⁴⁸⁶ Mishnah Avot 2:


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:51)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (תענית דף י"א) לעולם יראה אדם כאלו קדוש בתוך מעיו שנאמר (הושע י"ב ט') בקרבך קדוש, ולא זכה משה רבינו ע"ה לקבל התורה עד שהתענה ארבעים יום וארבעים לילה, ולא עלה אליהו זכור לטוב למרום עד שהתענה ארבעים יום וארבעים לילה, ולא זכה רבי אלעזר ורבי עקיבא ורבי ישמעאל לכל אותה החכמה והנבואה בעליתן לפרד"ס עד אשר התענו ימים רבים בכל מיני סגופים ופרישות. ולא זכה שלמה שנאמר בו (מלכים א' ה' י"א) ריחכם מכל האדם עד שהתענה ארבעים יום וארבעים לילה, כמו שאמרו על מ' של משלי שהיא מ"ם רבתי:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁴⁸⁷ “A person should always consider himself as if holiness dwells in his bowels, as written,⁴⁸⁸ ‘Holiness is in your innards.’” Our teacher, Moshe, peace be upon him, only merited receiving the Torah after having fasted for forty days and forty nights.⁴⁸⁹ Similarly, Eliyahu the Prophet, who is remembered for good, only merited ascending the heights after he fasted for forty days and forty nights.⁴⁹⁰ Likewise, Rabbi Elazar, Rabbi Akiva, and Rabbi Yishmael only merited all the wondrous wisdom and prophecy they attained when they ascended to the Pardes, after having fasted for many days and through all kinds of self-flagellation and abstention.⁴⁹¹ Similarly, King Solomon, about whom it states that,⁴⁹² “He was wiser than all men,” only merited to such wisdom after fasting for forty days and forty nights, as our Sages explained about the large letter *Mem*-מ⁴⁹³ of the opening word of Proverbs-*Mishlei*-משלי.⁴⁹⁴

footnotes:
⁴⁸⁷ Talmud Bavli, Taanit 11a-b
⁴⁸⁸ Hosea 11:9
⁴⁸⁹ Exodus 34:28
⁴⁹⁰ Kings I 19:8
⁴⁹¹ Heichalot Rabbati and Heichalot Zutrati
⁴⁹² Kings I 5:11
⁴⁹³ The numerical value of the letter mem-מ is forty.
⁴⁹⁴ Yalkut Shimoni Mishlei, Remez 929


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:52)


ובספר הזוהר (ח"א דף י"א ע"א) אמר רבי שמעון בר יוחאי לרבי פינחס כד חזייה דגופיה מלא חלודין בצאתו מן המערה אלו לא חמיתני בכך לא הייתי בכך.


It states in Zohar⁴⁹⁵ that when Rabbi Shimon bar Yochai came out of the cave, Rabbi Pinchas ben Ya’ir saw that his body was completely covered with blisters. He cried and said, “Woe is me, that I have seen you in such agony!” Rabbi Shimon bar Yochai answered him, “Great is your merit that you have seen me in such agony, for had you not seen me like this, I would not have merited to all that I merited.”

footnotes:
⁴⁹⁵ Zohar I 11a-b (See Ramak and the other commentaries.)


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:53)


ובפרשת תרומה (קמ"א ע"א) גופיהון דצדיקייא דלא אתמשכו בהאי עלמא בתר הנאות דההיא קליפה, לא שלטא עלייהו רוח מסאבא כלל, דכיון דלא אתענגו בהאי עלמא אלא מתענוגי דמצוה שבתין וחגין ההוא רוח מסאבא לא יכול לשלטאה עלייהו כלל כיון דלא אתענגו מדיליה כלום, ואי אתהני מדיליה ביומין דחול עליה אתמר (מלאכי ב' ג') וזריתי פרש על פניכם.


In Zohar Terumah it states,⁴⁹⁶ “The spirit of impurity has no dominion over the bodies of the righteous *Tzaddikim* who, in this world, were not drawn after the pleasures and lusts of that extraneous husk (*Kelipah*). For, they have not pleasured themselves in this world at all, except for fulfilling the *mitzvah* of pleasure on Shabbat and Biblical Festivals. The spirit of impurity thus has no dominion or hold on them at all, since they have not derived any pleasure whatsoever from that which is the opposite Holiness (*Sitra Achara*). However, about a person who did derive pleasure from the opposite the Holiness (*Sitra Achara*) during the mundane days of the week, the verse states,⁴⁹⁷ ‘I will smear dung upon your faces, the dung of your festive offerings, and you shall be carried out to its heap.’”

footnotes:
⁴⁹⁶ Zohar II 141a
⁴⁹⁷ Malachi 2:3


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:54)


ותנא דבי אליהו (א"ר פכ"ו) לא זכה אדם לתורה יבקש רחמים על אכילה ושתיה יתירה שלא תבא לתוך מעיו:


It was taught in the School of Eliyahu,⁴⁹⁸ “If a person has not yet merited to plead for mercy from *HaShem*-יהו״ה that Torah should be in his innards, he should at least plead for mercy that excess food and drink should not enter his innards.”

footnotes:
⁴⁹⁸ Tanna d’Vei Eliyahu Rabba, Ch. 26; Also see Ketubot 104a, Tosefot entitled “Lo Neheneiti – I did not derive any pleasure from even my pinky.”


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:55)


[תרגום - אמר רבי שמעון בן יוחאי לרבי פנחס כאשר ראה שגופו מלא פצעים בצאתו מן המערה, אילו לא ראיתני בכך, לא הייתי כך. ובפרשת תרומה גופם של הצדיקים שלא נמשכו בעוה"ז אחר הנאות דאותה קליפה, לא שולטת עליהם רוח טומאה כלל לפי שמאחר ולא התענגו בעוה"ז אלא מתענוגי מצוה שבתות וחגים אותו רוח טומאה לא יכול לשלוט עליהם כלל כיון שלא נהנו משלו כלום. ואם נהנה משלו בימות החול עליו נאמר וזרתי פרש על פניכם]:


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:56)


השררה והכבוד היא קשה מאד כי היא מביאה לאדם לידי הגאוה שהיא הקשה מכלם. ועל ידי כן יטיל אימה יתירה על הצבור שלא לשם שמים אשר סופו נעקר מן העולם וזרעו לא יצליח אחריו כלל,


**Power and honor** are extremely problematic, as they bring a person to haughtiness, which is worse than all the above-mentioned negative qualities. As a result of it, he becomes a leader who casts excessive fear on the community, in a manner that is not for the sake of the Name of *HaShem*-יהו״ה. As our sages, of blessed memory said, in the end he will be uprooted from the world and his children will be completely unsuccessful after him.⁴⁹⁹

footnotes:
⁴⁹⁹ See Talmud Bavli, Rosh HaShanah 17a


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:57)


כמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל (סנהדרין דף ק"א), וכמו שמצינו בירבעם החכם הגדול שעל ידי בקשת השררה נטרד הוא וזרעו מן העולם הזה ואין לו חלק לעולם הבא, ושאול בחיר ה' כבן שנה במלכו שלא טעם טעם חטא והיה נחבא אל הכלים בורח מן השררה וסופו נלכד ברשתה, ואמר (שמואל א' ט"ו ו') כבדני נא נגד זקני עמי, ורדף אחריה ואבד המלוכה ונהרג הוא ושלשת בניו, כנזכר לעיל במדת הקנאה:


We find this about the great sage, Yeravam, that because he sought power, both he and his descendants were uprooted from this world, nor does he have a portion in the coming world. Similarly, when Shaul, the chosen one of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, began his reign, he was as pure as a one-year-old child who has never tasted sin.⁵⁰⁰ Moreover, as the verse attests, when they sought to crown him as king,⁵⁰¹ “He hid amongst the baggage,” in that he fled from power. Nevertheless, he was ultimately caught in its net and pursued it, as he said to the Prophet Shmuel,⁵⁰² “Please honor me in the presence of the elders of my people.” In the end he lost the kingdom and he was killed along with three of his sons, as mentioned before in the section on envy.

footnotes:
⁵⁰⁰ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 22b
⁵⁰¹ Samuel I 10:22
⁵⁰² Samuel I 15:30


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:58)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ד משנה כ"א) הקנאה והתאוה והכבוד מוציאין את האדם מן העולם ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (ברכות דף נ"ה ע"א) הרבנות מקפחת וקוברת בעליה כי מביאתו לרום לבבו על קונו. כמו שכתוב (דברים י"ז ז') לבלתי רום לבבו וגו' למען יאריך ימים על ממלכתו.


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁰³ “Envy, lust, and [the desire for] honor, remove a person from the world.” Similarly, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁰⁴ “Authority shortens the life of those who possess it and buries them.” This is because it causes one’s heart to be haughty with his Maker, as the verse states,⁵⁰⁵ “So that his heart should not become exalted above his brethren and so that he will not depart from the commandment, neither to the right nor to the left, so that his years will be prolonged over his kingdom, he and his sons in the midst of Israel.”

footnotes:
⁵⁰³ Mishnah Avot 4:21
⁵⁰⁴ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 86b; Pesachim 87b; Avot d’Rabbi Natan Ch. 39
⁵⁰⁵ Deuteronomy 17:20


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:59)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (יומא דף פ"ו ע"ב, וסנהדרין דף ז' ע"ב) רב כד הוה נפיק לדינא הוה אמר ברעות נפשאי לקטלא נפיק וכו'. ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (מד"ר שמות פכ"ז) בני אם ערבת לרעך (משלי ו' א'), רב הונא פתר ליה במי שמתמנה פרנס על הצבור, מה כתיב בתריה, נוקשת באמרי פיך נלכדת באמרי פיך עשה זאת איפה בני והנצל, ואם לאו עליו כתיב (שם ל"ב) נואף אשה חסר לב משחית נפשו וגו' ואין מרחמים עליו בגיהנם,


Our sages, of blessed memory, said,⁵⁰⁶ “When Rava would go out to judge, he would say, ‘I willingly go out to die etc.’” Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁰⁷ “The verse states,⁵⁰⁸ ‘My son, if you have been a guarantor for your fellow etc.’ Rav Huna explained that this refers to a person who was appointed to a position of authority over the masses. However, the next verse continues,⁵⁰⁹ ‘You have been trapped by the words of your mouth, snared by the words of your mouth. Do this, therefore, my child, and be rescued; for you have come into your fellow’s hand. Go humble yourself etc.’” However, if he does not, about him the verse states,⁵¹⁰ “One who commits adultery with a married woman lacks an understanding heart, he who destroys his own soul will do this,” and they have no mercy upon him in purgatory (Gehinnom).

footnotes:
⁵⁰⁶ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 86b ibid.; Sanhedrin 7b
⁵⁰⁷ See Midrash Shemot Rabba 27:9, and elsewhere
⁵⁰⁸ Proverbs 6:1
⁵⁰⁹ Proverbs 6:2
⁵¹⁰ Proverbs 6:32


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:60)


אמר הקדוש ברוך הוא אני קדוש אני חסיד וכו' אם יש בך כל המדות האלו קבל עליך שררה ואם לאו אל תקבל. ודוד המלך ע"ה אמר (תהלים קל"א א') ה' לא גבה לבי וגו' נפשי כגמול עלי אמו:


The Holy One, blessed is He, said,⁵¹¹ “I am holy, I am pious, I am upright etc. If you possess all these qualities, you can accept a position of power and authority, but if not, do not accept it.” King David, peace be upon him, said,⁵¹² “*HaShem*-יהו״ה, my heart was not proud and my eyes were not haughty, nor did I pursue matters too great and too wondrous for me. I swear that I stilled and silenced my soul, like a suckling child at his mother’s side, my soul is like a suckling child.”

footnotes:
⁵¹¹ See Midrash Bamidbar Rabba 22:1; Tanchuma Matot 1:1
⁵¹² Psalms 131:1


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:61)


לכן הוי בורח מן השררה עד קצה האחרון וכמו שהארכתי במדת הגאוה, וכמו שאמרו בספר הזוהר (ח"ג קס"ה ע"א) זכאה איהו מאן דאזער גרמיה בהאי עלמא, ומי לנו גדול ממשה רבן של כל ישראל שסרב עם הקדוש ברוך הוא שבעת ימים ולא רצה ואמר (שמות ד' י"ג) שלח נא ביד תשלח עד שנאמר בו (שם י"ד) ויחר אף ה' במשה, ועשה בו רושם כמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל (שמות רבה פ"ג) ואחר כך נתרצה ולסוף נענש בעבורם במי מריבה ולא הועילו לו כל זכיותיו.


Therefore, flee from power and authority to the opposite extreme, as I explained at length when discussing the quality of pride. For, Zohar states,⁵¹³ “One who makes himself small in this world is meritorious.” Is there anyone greater than Moshe, the teacher of all Israel, who for seven days refused the authority placed upon him by the Holy One, blessed is He, and did not desire it, saying,⁵¹⁴ “Please, my Lord, send through whomever You will send!” About him the verse attests,⁵¹⁵ “The wrath of *HaShem*-יהו״ה burned against Moshe,” to the point that he was adversely affected by it, as our sages, of blessed memory, explained.⁵¹⁶ He subsequently assumed the responsibility, but in the end was punished because of the Jewish people at the waters of strife,⁵¹⁷ and all of his merits did not suffice to help him.

footnotes:
⁵¹³ Zohar III 168a
⁵¹⁴ Exodus 4:13
⁵¹⁵ Exodus 4:14
⁵¹⁶ Talmud Bavli, Zevachim 102a; Also see Rashi to Exodus 4:14 ibid.
⁵¹⁷ Deuteronomy 3:26 (see Rashi); Psalms 106:32


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:62)


כי כל המשתרר על הצבור אפילו לשם שמים כמשה סופו ליענש כי הוא ערב על כל מעשיהם כמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל (ספרי דברים פיסקא י"ג) על ואשימם בראשיכם. קל וחומר ליתמי כמונו שחס ושלום לא תהיה לנו תקומה:


For, whoever exerts power and authority over the congregation, even if it is for the sake of Heaven, like Moshe, will ultimately be punished. For, he is responsible for all their deeds, as our sages, of blessed memory, stated⁵¹⁸ in explanation of the verse,⁵¹⁹ “I will place them upon your heads.” How much more so does this certainly apply to orphans such as ourselves, for whom there would be no hope of salvation, God forbid.

footnotes:
⁵¹⁸ Midrash Dvarim Rabba 1:10
⁵¹⁹ Deuteronomy 1:13


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:63)


הוראת מעשיו לבריות. ידעת מה שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל (ירושלמי חגיגה פ"א) ענין אותה האשה שהיתה נענשת בגיהנם על שהיתה מספרת לחברתה היום התעניתי וכו'. ולא די שאינו מקבל שכר אלא שנדון בגיהנם כי הרי מגלה דעתו שאין מעשיו כלל לשם שמים והוא מכלל הפרושים (סוטה כ"ב ע"ב) פרוש נקפי, פרוש קזאי,


**Boastfulness**is a quality about which our sages, of blessed memory, related about the woman who was punished in purgatory (*Gehinnom*) because she told her friend, “Today I fasted etc.” That is, when a person boasts of his righteousness, not only does he not receive reward, but on the contrary, he is judged with the punishments of purgatory (*Gehinnom*). For, by boasting, he reveals his true intentions and that his actions are not at all for the sake of Heaven. He is counted amongst the seven categories of the pseudo-righteous (*Parushim*) who erode the world, such as those who practice self-flagellation and blood-letting etc.⁵²⁰

footnotes:
⁵²⁰ See Talmud Bavli, Sotah 22b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 4:64

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:64](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/4:64)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אליהו רבא פכ"א) לעולם יהא אדם ירא שמים בסתר ויהיו כל דרכיו לשם שמים ואם יכול אדם שיעשה כל דרכיו לשם שמים ולא יפרש לבריות אפילו אחד מהם, שכרו כפול ומכופל מפני שכבוד אלהים הסתר דבר:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵²¹ “A person should always be in a state of fear of Heaven, even in private, and all his ways should be for the sake of Heaven.” If a person is able, he should conduct himself in all his deeds and ways for the sake of Heaven, without revealing any of it to the creatures. His reward will be doubled and quadrupled, as the verse states,⁵²² “It is the honor of God to conceal a matter.”

footnotes:
⁵²¹ Tanna d’Vei Eliyahu Rabba, Ch. 21; Also see Mishnah Avot 2:12
⁵²² Proverbs 25:2


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:1)


השער החמישי:


Gate Five


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:2)


במדות האסורות:


**Forbidden Traits**


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:3)


לשון הרע אמרו רבותינו ז"ל (ערכין דף י"ד ע"ב) שקשה מעבודה זרה גלוי עריות ושפיכות דמים שהן החמורות מכל העברות, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (שם ט"ו ע"ב) כל המספר לשון הרע כופר בעיקר כמו שכתוב (תהלים י"ב ה') אשר אמרו ללשוננו נגביר שפתינו אתנו מי אדון לנו:


**Evil speech** is a quality about which our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵²³ “There are three transgressions that bring retribution against the one who perpetrates them in this world and disinherit him from a share in the coming world. These are idolatry, forbidden relations, and murder, but evil speech outweighs them all.” Our sages, of blessed memory, also stated,⁵²⁴ “Whoever speaks with an evil tongue denies the fundamental principle of the existence of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, as written,⁵²⁵ ‘Those who said, ‘Because of our tongues we shall prevail, our lips are with us, who is master over us?’’”

footnotes:
⁵²³ Talmud Bavli, Arachin 16b; Mishneh Torah, Hilchot De’ot 7:3
⁵²⁴ Talmud Bavli, Arachin 15b
⁵²⁵ Psalms 12:5


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:4)


רכילות הורג שלשה ושקול כשפיכות דמים ואינו נכנס בגן עדן, כמו שכתוב (תהלים ט"ו א') מי יגור באהליך וגו' לא רגל על לשונו. והנה דואג האדומי אביר הרועים ראש הסנהדרין, מפני הרכילות נטרד מן העולם הבא ונכרת בחצי ימיו וגרם הריגת אנשי נוב עיר הכהנים ומיתת שאול ובניו ובני ביתו:


**Gossip** kills three people and is equal to murder, and on account of it, one is barred from entry into the Garden of Eden. This is as written,⁵²⁶ “*HaShem*-יהו״ה, who may sojourn in Your tent? Who may dwell on Your Holy Mountain? One who walks in perfect innocence and does what is right, and who speaks the truth from his heart; who has no gossip on his tongue etc.” We see that Doeg the Edomite was one of the mighty shepherds of Israel and the head of the Sanhedrin.⁵²⁷ However, because of his gossip he was excised from the coming world, was killed having lived only half his days, and caused the people of Nov, the city of the Priests, to be killed, as well as the death of Shaul and his household.

footnotes:
⁵²⁶ Psalms 15:1
⁵²⁷ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 106b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:5)


הלבנת פנים, ומכנה שם רע לחבירו, והמתכבד בקלון חבירו, יורדין לגיהנם ואינם עולים ואין להם חלק לעולם הבא: והם בכלל לא תרצח, כמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל בפרק הזהב (ב"מ דף נ"ח ע"ב) במערבא במאי זהירי טפי, באזיל סומקא ואתי חורא:


**Causing embarrassment, calling one’s fellow by a negative nickname and glorying in the downfall one’s fellow** cause a person to descend to purgatory and never ascend, having no share in the coming world. These are included in the commandment,⁵²⁸ “You shall not murder.” This is as our sages, of blessed memory, taught,⁵²⁹ “Whoever humiliates another in public, it is as though he is spilling his blood. Over what *mitzvah* are they particularly vigilant in the West?⁵³⁰ In refraining from causing the color to leave a person’s face, so that his face becomes pale.”

footnotes:
⁵²⁸ Exodus 20:13
⁵²⁹ Talmud Bavli, Bava Metziya 58b
⁵³⁰ The land of Israel


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:6)


ליצנות היא אחת מארבע כתות שאינם מקבלות פני שכינה, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (עבודה זרה דף י"ח ע"ב) כל המתלוצץ נופל בגיהנם, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ג משנה ב') אפילו ישב בטל הרי זה מושב לצים וכמו שכתוב (תהלים א' א') אשרי האיש אשר לא הלך בעצת רשעים ובמושב לצים לא ישב וסמיך ליה כי אם בתורת ה' חפצו וגו', מה כתיב בתריה והיה כעץ שתול על פלגי מים וגו' לא כן הרשעים וגו',


**Levity** is one of the four classes of traits that prevent a person from receiving the countenance of the Indwelling Presence of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He – the *Shechinah*.⁵³¹ Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵³² “Whoever acts with levity falls into purgatory (*Gehinnom*).” Likewise, our sages, of blessed memory, stated⁵³³ that even if a person sits idly and not engaging in Torah study, it is considered as if he is sitting with the frivolous who have contempt for Torah, as it states,⁵³⁴ “Praiseworthy is the man who did not walk in the counsel of the wicked and did not stand in the path of the sinful and did not sit in the gathering of the frivolous.” The very next verse continues and states,⁵³⁵ “But his desire is in the Torah of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, and in His Torah he meditates day and night.” It continues,⁵³⁶ “He shall be like a tree planted alongside brooks of water, that yields its fruit in its season and whose leaf never withers; and everything that he does will succeed. Not so the wicked; Rather they are like the chaff that the wind drives away etc.”

footnotes:
⁵³¹ Talmud Bavli, Sotah 42a
⁵³² Talmud Bavli, Avodah Zarah 18b
⁵³³ Mishnah Avot 3:2
⁵³⁴ Psalms 1:1
⁵³⁵ Psalms 1:2
⁵³⁶ Psalms 1:3-4


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:7)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ו משנה ב') בכל יום בת קול יוצאת ואומרת אוי להם לבריות מעלבונה של תורה שכל מי שאינו עוסק בתורה נקרא נזוף, רצונו לומר מנודה לשמים. ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (סנהדרין דף ל"ט ע"ב) כי דבר ה' בזה וגו' (במדבר ט"ו ל"א) הכרת תכרת הנפש ההיא וגו', רבי נחמיה אומר זה כל מי שאפשר לו לעסוק בתורה ואינו עוסק, ואמר רב כל הפורש מדברי תורה ועוסק בדברי שיחה אש אוכלתו, וכל מי שמרפה עצמו מדברי תורה נופל בגיהנם ומאכילים אותו גחלי רתמים שנאמר (איוב פרקל' ד') הקוטפים מלוח עלי שיח, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ג מ"ה) המפנה לבו לבטלה הרי זה מתחיב בנפשו שנאמר (דברים ד' ט') רק השמר לך וגו':


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵³⁷ “Every day, a Heavenly voice (*Bat Kol*) goes forth from Mount Chorev and proclaims, ‘Woe to the creatures for their contempt of Torah.’ For, whosoever does not occupy himself in the study of Torah is called censured-*Nazuf*, meaning excommunicated from Heaven. Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵³⁸ “The following verse applies to such a person,⁵³⁹ ‘For he had contempt the word of *HaShem*-יהו״ה and violated His commandment, that soul shall surely be cut off – his sin is upon him.’ Rabbi Nechemiah says: This refers to anyone who is able to engage in Torah study but fails to do so.” Similarly,⁵⁴⁰ “Rav said: “Whoever excuses himself from studying the words of Torah, a fire consumes him.” Likewise, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁴¹ “Whoever weakens or stops his study of words of Torah to engage in idle chatter, will be fed the hot coals of a Juniper tree, as written,⁵⁴² ‘They pluck saltwort and the leaves of wormwood and the roots of the Juniper tree is their bread.’” Our sages, of blessed memory, likewise stated,⁵⁴³ “One who turns his heart to idle matters is mortally guilty,” as written,⁵⁴⁴ “Only beware for yourself and greatly beware for your soul, lest you forget the words⁵⁴⁵ that your eyes have beheld and lest you remove them from your heart all the days of your life etc.”

footnotes:
⁵³⁷ Mishnah Avot 6
⁵³⁸ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 99b; Yalkut Shimoni Remez 749
⁵³⁹ Numbers 15:31
⁵⁴⁰ Talmud Bavli, Bava Batra 79a
⁵⁴¹ Talmud Bavli, Chagigah 12b; Ketubot 111b; Brachot 63a and elsewhere.
⁵⁴² Job 30:3
⁵⁴³ Mishnah Avot 3:4
⁵⁴⁴ Deuteronomy 4:9
⁵⁴⁵ The word “Dvarim-דברים” is generally translated as “things” but also means “words” and in the above verse refers to the words of Torah and the Ten Commandments (which are called (Exodus 34:28) “Aseret HaDvarim-עשרת הדברים”), as stated in the continuing verse (Deuteronomy 4:10), “The day that you stood before HaShem-יהו״ה, your God, at Chorev.”


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:8)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (סנהדרין דף ז' ע"א) אין תחלת דינו של אדם אלא על דברי תורה שנאמר (משלי י"ז י"ד) פוטר מים ראשית מדון, וכתיב (יהושע א' ח') והגית בו יומם ולילה כי אז תצליח את דרכך ואז תשכיל, וכל רגע, שעה, ויום שאדם בטל מדברי תורה עליו נאמר (קהלת א' ט"ו) וחסרון לא יוכל להמנות,


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁴⁶ “The beginning of a person’s judgment is that he is judged only concerning the study of Torah, as written,⁵⁴⁷ ‘Exemption from waters is the beginning of judgment.’”⁵⁴⁸ Similarly, it is written,⁵⁴⁹ “This Book of the Torah shall not leave from your mouth; you shall contemplate it day and night, so that you may guard yourself to do according to all that is written in it; for then your way will be successful, and then you will be wise.” About each moment, hour or day that a person is idle from studying the words of Torah, it is written,⁵⁵⁰ “Crookedness that cannot be straightened, lacking that is incalculable.”

footnotes:
⁵⁴⁶ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 7a
⁵⁴⁷ Proverbs 17:14
⁵⁴⁸ The term “release-Poter-פוטר” here means that he exempts himself from the waters, which refers to Torah study. The term “Madon-מדון” which means “strife,” is also of the root “judgement-Din-דין” as the Talmud here indicates. Thus, the verse is understood to mean, “Excusing oneself from the waters of Torah study is the beginning of judgment.” (See Rashi to Talmud Bavli, Kiddushin 40b.)
⁵⁴⁹ Joshua 1:8
⁵⁵⁰ Ecclesiastes 1:15 (See Rashi)


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:9)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (פאה פ"א משנה א') אלו דברים שאדם עושה אותם אוכל מפירותיהם בעולם הזה והקרן קימת לו לעולם הבא וכו' ותלמוד תורה כנגד כולם, ולא עוד אלא שהעוסק בתלמוד תורה פטור מכל המצות שהיא שקולה ככל המצות:


On the other hand, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁵¹ “These are the things, the fruits of which a person enjoys in this world, while the principle remains for the coming world; honoring one’s father and mother, doing deeds of kindness, early attendance in the house of study morning and evening, welcoming guests, visiting the sick, dowering a bride, escorting the dead, concentration in prayer, bringing peace between man and his fellow and between husband and wife. But the study of Torah is equal to them all.” Moreover, whoever is engaged in Torah study is exempt from all the commandments, since it equals all the commandments.⁵⁵²

footnotes:
⁵⁵¹ Mishnah Pe’ah 1:1
⁵⁵² Talmud Bavli, Sukkah 25a-26b; Brachot 11a, 16a; Mishneh Torah, Hilchot Ishut (Laws of Marriage) 15:2, and elsewhere.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:10)


ותנא דבי אליהו זכור לטוב (עבודה זרה דף ה' ע"ב) אשרי אדם שמשים עצמו כשור לעול וכחמור למשאוי ויושב וקורא ושונה בתורה בכל יום תמיד ומיד רוח הקודש שורה עליו, ואם היושב בטל נענש כל כך, קל וחומר למי שמתלוצץ ממש:


It was taught in the school of Eliyahu, who is remembered for good,⁵⁵³ “Happy is the man who subjugates himself to Torah study, like an ox to a yoke and like a donkey to a burden, sitting and studying and reviewing Torah every day constantly. The Holy Spirit (*Ru’ach HaKodesh*) will immediately rest upon him.” If this is the case, that when a person sits idly he is punished to such a great degree, then how much greater is the punishment of a person who actually behaves with levity and disdain toward Torah.

footnotes:
⁵⁵³ Tanna d’Bei Eliyahu Zuta, Ch. 1; Talmud Bavli, Avodah Zarah 5b


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:11)


שיחה בטלה גורמת לידי עבירות גדולות, כי הרי אפילו ישב בטל הרי זה מושב לצים ונענש ענשים גדולים, כנזכר לעיל בענין הליצנות, מכל שכן השח שיחות בטילות בפועל, ולא עוד שמביא לידי לשון הרע ורכילות וליצנות ומספר בגנות חבירו:


**Idle chatter**leads to very great sins. For, even when he merely sits idly it is considered as though he sits with the frivolous who have contempt for Torah, and he is punished with very great punishments, as we mentioned before regarding the matter of levity and mockery. This being so, how much more is this true of one who actually engages in empty chatter. Moreover, this brings him to come to speak badly of others, to gossip, slander and mock others.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:12)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (יומא דף י"ט ע"ב) השח שיחה בטלה עובר בעשה שנאמר (דברים ו' ז') ודברת בם, ואמרו על רבן יוחנן בן זכאי (סוכה דף כ"ח ע"א) ועל רב (רמב"ם פרק ב' מהלכות הלכה ט') שמימיהם לא שחו שיחת חולין, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"א מי"ז) לא מצאתי לגוף טוב אלא שתיקה, וכתיב (מיכה ג' כ"ח) ישב בדד וידום יתן בעפר פיהו, וכתיב (משלי י"ח כ"א) מות וחיים ביד לשון, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ג מי"ג) סיג לחכמה שתיקה, ואמר שלמה (קהלת ה' א') אל תבהל על פיך ולבך אל ימהר להוציא דבר לפני האלהים כי האלהים בשמים ואתה על הארץ על כן יהיו דבריך מעטים:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁵⁴ “One who engages in idle chatter violates a positive commandment, as written,⁵⁵⁵ ‘You shall speak of them,’ referring to words of Torah, rather than other matters.” It was said about Rabbi Yochanan ben Zachai,⁵⁵⁶ that in all his days he never engaged in idle conversation. Our sages, of blessed memory, likewise coined this adage,⁵⁵⁷ “If a word is worth one *sela*, silence is worth two.” Similarly, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁵⁸ “In all my days I grew up among the sages and I found nothing better for a person than silence.” It is written,⁵⁵⁹ “Let one sit in solitude and be silent,” and it states,⁵⁶⁰ “Death and life are in the hands of the tongue.” Our sages, of blessed memory, also said,⁵⁶¹ “The guardrail of wisdom is silence.” King Solomon said,⁵⁶² “Be not rash with your mouth, and let not your heart be hasty in uttering a word before God; for God is in heaven and you are on earth, so let your words be few.”

footnotes:
⁵⁵⁴ Talmud Bavli, Yoma 19b
⁵⁵⁵ Deuteronomy 6:7
⁵⁵⁶ Talmud Bavli, Sukkah 28a
⁵⁵⁷ Talmud Bavli, Megillah 18a
⁵⁵⁸ Mishnah Avot 1:17
⁵⁵⁹ Lamentations 3:28
⁵⁶⁰ Proverbs 18:21
⁵⁶¹ Mishnah Avot 3:13
⁵⁶² Ecclesiastes 5:1


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:13)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (חולין דף פ"ט) האמנם אלם צדק תדברון (תהילים נ״ח:ב׳) מה אומנותו של אדם בעולם הזה ישים עצמו אלם, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (חגיגה כ"ג ע"א) רבי כד הוה מטי להאי קרא הוה בכי (עמוס ד׳:י״ג) כי הנה יוצר הרים ובורא רוח ומגיד לאדם מה שיחו, אפילו שיחה קלה שאדם משיח עם אשתו ואפילו דברים שאין בהם ממש הם נכתבים על פנקסו של אדם וקוראים לפניו בשעת מיתתו:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁶³ “The verse states,⁵⁶⁴ ‘Is there truly (*HaUmnam*-ה**אמנ**ם) silence (*Eilem*-אלם) when you should be speaking righteousness?’ That is, in what skill (*Umanut*-**אמנ**ות) should a person toil in this world? He should toil in making himself as if he is mute (*Eelem*-אלם).” Similarly, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁶⁵ “When the Master would read the following verse, he would cry. The verse states,⁵⁶⁶ ‘For behold, He Who forms the mountains and creates the wind, and declares to man what is his speech.’ This even includes the lighthearted and frivolous speech between a man and his wife before engaging in relations. Even if they are completely insignificant, they nevertheless written into a person’s ledger and read before him at the time of his death.”

footnotes:
⁵⁶³ Talmud Bavli, Chullin 89a
⁵⁶⁴ Psalms 58:2
⁵⁶⁵ Talmud Bavli, Chagigah 23a
⁵⁶⁶ Amos 4:13


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:14)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל שתיקתו של אדם מביאתו לידי ענוה, והענוה היא ראש ליראת שמים, ובשעה שאינו עוסק בתורה יהיה דומם ומדביק שפתותיו זו לזו כשתי ריחים ששוכבות זו על זו מפני שצדקותיו וזכיותיו של אדם כולם אינם מספיקות במה שיוציא בשפתיו, אשרי מי שישים עצמו אלם וחרש חגר וסומא, וכל כך למה כדי שלא יחטא ויחייב עצמו ואת איבריו לגיהנם, וכן דוד הוא אומר (תהלים ל"א י"א) ואני כחרש לא אשמע וכאלם לא יפתח פיו:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁶⁷ “One’s silence leads to humility,” and humility the chief quality that leads to fear of Heaven.⁵⁶⁸ Thus, when engaging in the study of Torah, he should be silent and glue his lips together, like two millstones that sit upon each other. This is because all the righteousness and meritoriousness a person accrues are insufficient from saving him from what comes out of his lips. Happy is he who makes himself as if mute and like a deaf person, like a cripple and one who is blind! Why is it imperative to take such great care? So that he should not come to sin and obligate himself and his limbs, thus condemning himself to purgatory (*Gehinnom*). Similarly, King David said,⁵⁶⁹ “I am like a deaf man, I do not hear, like a mute who does not open his mouth.”

footnotes:
⁵⁶⁷ Mishnah Sotah 9:15; Also see Tanchuma Vayeitze 6; Tzava’at HaRivash, translated as The Way of The Baal Shem Tov, Ch. 49.
⁵⁶⁸ Talmud Bavli, Avodah Zarah 20b
⁵⁶⁹ Psalms 38:14


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:15)


ובספר הזוהר (ח"ב נ"ט ע"א) לית לך הבל דלית ליה קלא, וכל אנון מלין דאשתדל בהו בר נש דלאו אינון לפלחנא דקודשא בריך הוא ההיא מלה אתעביד מנה הבל ושטאת בעלמא, וכד נפקא נשמתיה ההוא הבל מגלגלא ליה בעלמא, ודא איהו (שמואל א כ״ה:כ״ט) ואת נפש אויביך יקלענה בתוך כף הקלע, ואמרו שם (ח"ג קפ"ג ע"ב) ותלבש אסתר מלכות אוליפנא כל מאן דנטיר פומיה ולישניה זכי לאתלבשא ברוח קודשא:


In Zohar it states,⁵⁷⁰ “There is not a single breath that does not possess a voice and create a spirit. All the words that a person speaks which are not for the sake of serving the Holy One, blessed is He, create a spirit that carries and travels around in the world and when the soul leaves him upon death, that spirit is hurled at him in the torments of the grave, like a stone flung from a slingshot (*Kaf HaKela*). This is the meaning of the verse,⁵⁷¹ ‘may He hurl away the soul of your enemies as one shoots a stone from a slingshot (*Kaf HaKela*).’” It also states in Zohar,⁵⁷² “From the verse,⁵⁷³ ‘And Esther donned royal garments,’ we learn that any person who guards his mouth and tongue, merits to don the garment of the spirit of holiness and Divine Inspiration (*Ru’ach HaKodesh*).’”⁵⁷⁴

footnotes:
⁵⁷⁰ Zohar II 59a
⁵⁷¹ Samuel I 25:29
⁵⁷² Zohar III 183b
⁵⁷³ Esther 5:1
⁵⁷⁴ Just as Esther guarded her mouth (Esther 2:20) “and did not reveal her kindred or her people, as Mordechai had instructed her.”


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:16)


[תרגום - אין לך הבל שאין לו קול וכל אותן מילים שמשתדל בהם בן אדם שאינם לעבודת הקדוש ברוך הוא אותה מילה נעשה ממנה הבל ומסתובבת בעולם, וכאשר יוצאת נשמתו אותו הבל מגלגל אותו בעולם. וזה הוא מה שנאמר ואת נפש אויבך יקלענה בתוך כף הקלע. ואמרו שם ותלבש אסתר מלכות למדנו שכל מי ששומר פיו ולשונו זוכה להתלבש ברוח הקודש]:


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:17)


המסתכל בעריות אפילו יהיה כמשה יד ליד לא ינקה מדינה של גיהנם. וסופו לחטוא בעריות ומגרה יצר הרע בעצמו ויבוא לידי קרי החמור כמו שיתבאר בשער ו':


**One who gazes at women,**⁵⁷⁵ even if he literally is equal in stature to Moshe, he will not be cleansed of the judgments of purgatory (*Gehinnom*). In the end he will come to sin with forbidden relations. He rouses the lusts of his evil inclination within himself, and will ultimately come to wasteful seminal emissions, the gravity of which will be explained in the sixth gate.

footnotes:
⁵⁷⁵ See Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 92a; Mishneh Torah, Hilchot Teshuvah 4:4


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:18)


הנהנה בכבוד התורה נוטל חייו מן העולם ומתחייב בנפשו, ומעשה דרבי טרפון יוכיח (נדרים דף ס"ב ע"א) שהיו משליכין אותו ליאור להורגו ולא רצה ליהנות מדברי תורה, כמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"ד מ"ה) אל תעשם עטרה להתגדל בהם ולא קרדום וכו', שכל הנהנה מכבוד תורה נוטל חייו מן העולם וסופו יורש גיהנם:


**One who derives benefit from the honor of the Torah** removes his life from the world and is liable of the death penalty. The incident with Rabbi Tarfon proves this.⁵⁷⁶ Namely, “A certain man found Rabbi Tarfon eating figs when most of the knives had already been set aside.⁵⁷⁷ He placed Rabbi Tarfon in a sack, lifted him up and carried him to throw him into the river. Rabbi Tarfon cried out and said, ‘Woe to Tarfon, for this man is killing him.’ When the man heard that it was Rabbi Tarfon, he left him and ran away. Rabbi Abahu said in the name of Rabbi Chananiyah ben Gamliel: All the days of that righteous man he was distressed over this matter, saying, ‘Woe is me, for I made use of the crown of Torah.’” As our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁷⁸ “Do not make them into a crown for self-exaltation, nor a spade with which to dig… Whoever derives worldly benefit from the words of Torah, removes his life from the world.” In the end, such a person inherits purgatory (*Gehinnom*).

footnotes:
⁵⁷⁶ Talmud Bavli, Nedarim 62a
⁵⁷⁷ That is, Rabbi Tarfon assumed that the figs were abandoned. (Rashi to Nedarim 62a ibid.)
⁵⁷⁸ Mishnah Avot 4


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:19)


השקר. המדבר שקרים כופר באלהי ישראל ומודה באל אחר שהוא שקר. והוא מכת שאינו רואה פני שכינה כמו שכתוב (תהלים ק"א ז') דובר שקרים לא יכון לנגד עיני, ואינו בכלל ישראל שכתוב בהם (צפניה ג' י"ג) שארית ישראל לא יעשו עולה ולא ידברו כזב, וימיו מתקצרים,


**Falsehood.** A person who speaks falsehood denies the God of Israel, giving credence to another god, called falsehood. A person such as this is in the category of those who shall not greet the countenance of the Indwelling Presence of *HaShem*-*יהו״ה*, blessed is He, the *Shechinah*. This is as stated,⁵⁷⁹ “One who tells lies shall not be established before My eyes.” A person such as this is not considered to be included amongst the Jewish people, about whom it is written,⁵⁸⁰ “The remnant of Israel will not commit corruption, they will not speak falsehood, and a deceitful tongue will not be found in their mouth,” and the lifespan of such a person will be shortened.

footnotes:
⁵⁷⁹ Psalms 101:7
⁵⁸⁰ Zephaniah 3:13


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:20)


אך הדובר אמת מאריך ימים כמו שאמרו במסכת סנהדרין (דף צ"ז ע"א) על רב טביומי דאילו הוו יהבי ליה כל חללי דעלמא לא הוה משני בדבוריה. זמנא חדא איקלע לההוא אתר דקושטא שמיה דלא הוו משנו בדבורם ולא הוה מיית איניש בלא זמניה וכו' שכיבו ליה תרתין בנין, אמרו ליה, במטותא מינך פוק מהכא ולא תגרי בנא מותנא.


In contrast, one who speaks truthfully lengthens his lifespan, as stated in Tractate Sanhedrin⁵⁸¹ about Rav Tavyomee that, “Even if they were to give him the entire world, his words would not deviate from the truth. Rava said: “Once, I happened to come to a certain place called Truth, the inhabitants of which never deviated from truth in their words, and no one ever died prematurely there. I married a woman there and she bore me two sons.” One day his wife was sitting and washing her hair and her neighbor came and knocked on the door. He thought, ‘It is improper to say that she is bathing,’ and he instead said, ‘She is not here.’ Because of this, his two sons died. The people of the place came and asked him, “What is the meaning of this?” He answered, explaining the nature of the incident. They said, “Please leave our place and do not provoke premature death upon these people.”

footnotes:
⁵⁸¹ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 97a


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:21)


וכתיב (ירמיה ט' ד') למדו לשונם דבר שקר וגו' העל אלה לא אפקד בם נאם ה' אם בגוי אשר כזה לא תתנקם נפשי.


It is written,⁵⁸² “Each man lies to his fellow and they do not speak truth; they train their tongue to speak falsehood, striving to be iniquitous. Your dwelling is amid deceit; because of their deceit they refuse to know Me – the word of *HaShem*-*יהו״ה*. Therefore, thus said *HaShem Tzva’ot*-*יהו״ה צבאו״ת*, I shall smelt them and I shall test them – for what [else] can I do for the daughter of My people? Their tongue is like a drawn arrow, speaking deceit; with his mouth a person speaks peace with his fellow, but within himself, he lays his ambush. Shall I not punish them for these things? – the word of *HaShem*-*יהו״ה*. From a nation such as this, shall My soul not exact vengeance?”

footnotes:
⁵⁸² Jeremiah 9:4-8


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:22)


ולא נענשו המרגלים ודור המדבר אלא מפני עדות שקר, ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (סנהדרין צ"ב ע"א) כל המחליף בדבורו כאלו עובד עבודה זרה. וכתיב (משלי ו' ט"ז) שש הנה שנא ה' וגו' עינים רמות, לשון שקר ויפיח כזבים וגו', וכתיב (תהילים ל״ד:י״ג) מי האיש החפץ חיים וגו' נצור לשונך מרע.


The spies and the generation of the desert were only punished because of false testimony. Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁸³ “Whoever speaks deceitfully, it is as though he worships idolatry.” It is written,⁵⁸⁴ “*HaShem*-*יהו״ה* hates these six, but the seventh is the abomination of His soul. Haughty eyes, a false tongue and hands that spill innocent blood. A heart that plots iniquitous thoughts, feet that run to do evil, a false witness who spouts lies, and one who stirs up strife among brothers.” The verse states,⁵⁸⁵ “They do not speak truth… Shall I not punish them for these things?” It also is written,⁵⁸⁶ “Who is the man who desires life, who loves days of seeing good? Guard your tongue from speaking evil, and your lips from speaking deceit.”

footnotes:
⁵⁸³ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 92a (See Rashi)
⁵⁸⁴ Proverbs 6:16-19
⁵⁸⁵ Jeremiah 9:4-8
⁵⁸⁶ Psalms 34:13


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:23)


גחזי לא נטרד אלא מפני ששקר בשם אלישע. אמנון כזב לתמר ונהרג, רוחו של נבות נטרד ממחיצתו על שדיבר כזב שנאמר (מלכים א כ״ב:כ׳) מי יפתה את אחאב וגו' צא, צא ממחיצתך,


Gechazi was only punished because he lied in the name of Elisha.⁵⁸⁷ Amnon deceived Tamar, which led to him being killed.⁵⁸⁸ The spirit of Navot was sent out from before the Presence of *HaShem*-*יהו״ה*, blessed is He, because he spoke deceitfully,⁵⁸⁹ as it states,⁵⁹⁰ “And *HaShem*-*יהו״ה* said, ‘Who will lure Achav to go up [to war] that he may fall in Ramoth-Gilead?’ This one said, ‘Like this’ and this one said, ‘Like this!’ Then a spirit came forward and stood before *HaShem*-*יהו״ה* and said, ‘I shall lure him!’ And *HaShem*-*יהו״ה* said, ‘How?’ [The spirit] replied, ‘I will go out and be a spirit of falsehood in the mouths of all his prophets.’ He said, ‘You will lure him and you will succeed. Go out (*Tzei*-*צא*) and do so!’” What is the meaning of the term “Go out-*Tzei*-*צא*?” It means, “Get out of My presence!”⁵⁹¹

footnotes:
⁵⁸⁷ Kings II 5:20-27; Midrash Tanchuma, Metzora 1:1
⁵⁸⁸ Samuel II 13:1-29
⁵⁸⁹ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 102b; Zohar I 192b (Kings I 21:1-13)
⁵⁹⁰ Kings 1 22:20-22
⁵⁹¹ Talmud Bavli, Sanhedrin 102b ibid.; Zohar I 192b ibid. – That is, he was a liar in the first instance, and was thus expelled from the presence of HaShem-יהו״ה, blessed is He.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:24)


ואמר רב הונא לא גלו ישראל מארצם אלא על שעברו על פסוק (שמות כ"ג ז') מדבר שקר תרחק. ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (שבת דף קי"ט ע"ב, חגיגה דף י"ד ע"ב) לא חרבה ירושלים אלא על שפסקו ממנה בעלי אמנה שנאמר (ירמיה ה' א') שוטטו בחוצות ירושלים.


Rav Huna said,⁵⁹² “The Jewish people were only exiled from their land because they transgressed the verse,⁵⁹³ ‘Distance yourself from a false word.’” Similarly, our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁹⁴ “Jerusalem was not destroyed until trustworthy men ceased to exist in it, as it states,⁵⁹⁵ ‘Walk about in the streets of Jerusalem, see now and know, and seek in its plazas; if you will find a man, if there is one who dispenses justice and seeks truth, then I will forgive her.’”

footnotes:
⁵⁹² Reishit Chochmah, Shaar HaKedushah, Ch. 12
⁵⁹³ Exodus 23:7
⁵⁹⁴ Talmud Bavli, Chagigah 14a
⁵⁹⁵ Jeremiah 5:1


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:25)


ובספר הזוהר בזמנא דשקר דאיהו סמא"ל שלטא כתיב (דניאל ח' י"ב) ותשלך אמת ארצה. ודא גרים חרבן בית ראשון ושני.


It states in Zohar,⁵⁹⁶ “About a time in which the Jewish people speak falsely about the Torah of Truth, the verse states,⁵⁹⁷ ‘Truth was hurled to the ground.’ It is about such a time of exile that the verse states,⁵⁹⁸ ‘Truth became lacking,’ and falsehood reigns in the world. What is falsehood? That is *Sama’el*. This is what caused the destruction of both the first and second Holy Temples.”

footnotes:
⁵⁹⁶ Tikkunei Zohar, Tikkun 63, 95a
⁵⁹⁷ Daniel 8:12
⁵⁹⁸ Isaiah 59:15


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:26)


ואמרו רבותינו ז"ל (אבות פ"א מי"ח) על שלשה דברים העולם עומד על הדין ועל האמת ועל השלום, וכתיב (תהלים ט"ו א') ה' מי יגור באהליך וגו'. ודובר אמת בלבבו, אף מה שחשב בלבבו צריך לאמת אותו במעשה, כמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל בעובדא דרב ספרא, וכתיב (מלאכי א') תורת אמת היתה בפיהו ועולה לא נמצא בשפתיו כי מלאך ה' צבאות הוא:


Our sages, of blessed memory, stated,⁵⁹⁹ “The world stands on three things: On Justice, Truth and Peace.” It is written,⁶⁰⁰ “Who may sojourn in Your tent? One who speaks the truth in his heart.” That is, even when it comes to matters that he only thought in his heart, he must bring forth into action and actualize them truthfully, in accordance what our sages,⁶⁰¹ of blessed memory, taught regarding the incident with Rav Safra. It is written,⁶⁰² “The Torah of Truth was in his mouth, and injustice was not found on his lips… for he is an angel of *HaShem Tzva’ot*-יהו״ה צבאו״ת.”

footnotes:
⁵⁹⁹ Mishnah Avot 1:18
⁶⁰⁰ Psalms 15:1-2
⁶⁰¹ Talmud Bavli, Bava Batra 88a; Makkot 24a, and Rashi there, citing She’eltot d’Rav Acha (She’ilta 36) – The incident with Rav Safra is that he had placed an object for sale. A man came before him to purchase it when he was in the middle of reciting the Shema. The man named his price, but Rav Safra did not respond because he was in the middle of the Shema. The man thought that Rav Safra was not interested in selling it at that price, so he raised his purchase price. When Rav Safra concluded the Shema, he told the man that he would accept the original price, for that is the price that he was originally satisfied with. It is about him that the verse states (Psalms 15:2), “One who speaks the truth in his heart” is applied.
⁶⁰² Malachi 2:6


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:27)


החנף הוא מן ארבע כתות שאינן מקבלות פני שכינה, לפי שמזלזל בכבוד המקום בהחניפו את הרשע, וכמו שכתוב (משלי כ"ד כ"ד) אומר לרשע צדיק אתה יקבוהו עמים יזעמוהו לאומים, ואפילו שמחניף למי שאינו רשע הרי הוא כמתירא ובוטח באדם, וכתיב (ירמיהו י״ז:ה׳) ארור הגבר אשר יבטח באדם, וכתיב (איוב י״ג:ט״ז) לא לפניו חנף יבוא,


**The flatterer**is amongst the four classes of people who shall not greet the countenance of the Indwelling Presence of *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He (the *Shechinah*).⁶⁰³ This is because he denigrates the honor of the Ever Present One, *HaShem*-יהו״ה, blessed is He, when he cajoles and flatters the wicked. This is as written,⁶⁰⁴ “One who tells a wicked person, ‘You are righteous,’ – the peoples shall curse him, the nations shall despise him.” Even if he flatters and cajoles a person who is not wicked, it nevertheless is as if he fears man and places his trust in man, as it is written,⁶⁰⁵ “Accursed is the man who puts his trust in people.” Regarding such a person the verse states,⁶⁰⁶ “The flatterer shall not come before Him.”

footnotes:
⁶⁰³ Talmud Bavli, Sotah 42a
⁶⁰⁴ Proverbs 24:24
⁶⁰⁵ Jeremiah 17:5
⁶⁰⁶ Job 13:16; See Talmud Bavli, Sotah 42a ibid.


###### Sha'arei Kedusha, Part 2 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sha%27arei%20Kedusha/s/Part%202/r/5:28)


וכמו שאמרו רבותינו ז"ל (סנהדרין דף י"ט ע"א) בעובדא דינאי המלך שהחניפו לו הסנהדרין בדבר מועט שלא העמידוהו בשעת הדין ובא גבריאל וחבטן בקרקע ומתו, גם כשהחניפו לאגריפס המלך שהיה צדיק נתחייבו שונאיהם של ישראל כליה,
